# Y1V01 — Volume 1

## Volume thesis

The school, Horikita, and Ayanokōji introduce competing theories of ability. The school hides the criteria through which freedom becomes collective judgment; Horikita treats current academic weakness as evidence of disposability; Ayanokōji begins testing a contextual and developmental account of `実力` while already controlling the visibility and attribution of his own interventions.

## Revision status

The prior first-pass analysis was broadly confirmed but qualified: Ayanokōji does not merely refuse institutional measurement; he authors the data and causal story that others are permitted to observe.


# Y1V02 — Volume 2

## Volume thesis

Volume 2 expands the Year 1 problem from hidden evaluation to **control over legibility**. Truth, ability, private identity, and danger do not become politically effective merely by existing; they must become visible in a form another person or institution will recognize. Sudō lacks credibility despite telling the truth, Sakura controls access to different versions of herself, Ichinose converts trust into cross-class authority, Horikita learns to operationalize a deliberately seeded idea, and Ayanokōji begins to engineer not only outcomes but the conditions under which other people decide what is believable.

## Revision status

The prior first-pass formulation of Volume 2 as a study of truth, proof, and credibility is retained but sharpened. The second pass distinguishes event truth, evidentiary capacity (`証拠能力`), probative force (`証明力`), credibility, and the institutionally actionable record. Sakura's Shizuku identity is reclassified from a simple false-mask/true-self opposition into authored visibility; Horikita's role in the dummy-camera plan is revised from passive receipt to **seeded discovery plus autonomous operationalization**; and Ayanokōji's delayed intervention in the stalker incident receives stronger ethical scrutiny.

# Y1V03 — Volume 3

## Volume thesis

Volume 3 relocates `実力` from the isolated individual into the relationship among person, environment, and collective. The island exam makes heterogeneous competence valuable only when it can be recognized, coordinated, or converted into action; Class D's ordinary progress therefore comes from increasingly usable distributed ability. Its first-place result, however, is secretly produced by Ayanokōji's more troubling capacity to author the environment in which others act, linking the volume's freedom motif to coercive developmental design.

## Revision status

The first-pass account of **freedom under coercion**, **false teamwork**, and Ayanokōji's emergence as hidden ruler is retained but refined. The second pass makes **ecological/socially usable ability** the deeper organizing problem; distinguishes low-coercion scaffolding, ordinary relational encouragement, and coercive environmental authorship; strengthens Hirata's role as service leader whose universal-happiness ideal contains an unresolved contradiction; preserves Chabashira's father-contact claim as locally unresolved; and continues to classify Ayanokōji's possible authorship of the manual fire as strong inference rather than textual fact. `偽りのチームワーク` is sharpened into real cooperation + fragile trust + false causal attribution.

# Y1V04 — Volume 4

## Volume thesis

Volume 4 turns trust into an exchangeable political resource and moves Ayanokōji from controlling public legibility and exam environments toward controlling the conditions of private dependence. The zodiac examination prices disclosure, concealment, and betrayal, while the Kei operation makes protection, evidence, trauma, and coercion into the infrastructure of a hidden collaboration. The volume's central ethical problem is not whether Ayanokōji's protection works, but what authority effective protection acquires when the protector helped manufacture the danger and narrow the subject's alternatives.

## Revision status

The first-pass emphasis on protection, dependency, betrayal, and Kei's recruitment is retained but materially sharpened. `寄生虫` is classified as Kei's trauma-authored self-theory rather than objective ontology; Hirata's refusal of exclusive retaliation becomes ethical strength as well as practical limitation; Ichinose emerges as a consensual but strategically sophisticated leader; the phone plan becomes layered identity architecture rather than a simple swap; and Ayanokōji's primary objective is revised from immediate exam gain toward acquisition of future social infrastructure. The operation against Kei is classified directly as coercive recruitment involving deliberate non-rescue, sexualized threat, bodily privacy violation, and genuine later protective benefit.


# Planned append order

`Y1V04.5 → Y1V05 → Y1V06 → Y1V07 → Y1V07.5 → Y1V08 → Y1V09 → Y1V10 → Y1V11 → Y1V11.5 → Y1FF`

# Y1V04.5 — Volume 4.5

## Volume thesis

Volume 4.5 functions as Year 1's first sustained **counter-curriculum**. With no major special examination defining success, ordinary life reveals which capacities, attachments, preferences, and ethical choices emerge when points and rank recede from the foreground. It also demonstrates that Ayanokōji already possesses both low-coercion and coercive modes of intervention, making the question increasingly not whether he can help without total control, but when he chooses to do so.

## Revision status

The prior interpretation of Volume 4.5 as ordinary-life developmental space is strengthened. The principal second-pass correction concerns Operation Delta: Ayanokōji prevents durable retention and distribution of the changing-room footage, but knowingly allows unauthorized live surveillance and recording to begin as part of his covert sabotage. The protective outcome therefore does not erase ethical complicity in method.

## Revision status

Volume 5 strengthens the multidimensional `実力` model, records the first public exposure of a major portion of Ayanokōji's physical ability, and creates a direct within-volume comparison between low-coercion scaffolding and deliberate developmental nonintervention. The cumulative evidence count through Y1V05 is 352 entries.


# Y1V06 — Volume 6

## Volume thesis

Volume 6 asks whether collective strength is built through removal of difficult members or through distributed structures that can integrate unequal, conflicted people. Paper Shuffle makes academic survival a problem of pairing, teaching, information, and class coordination; Kushida turns trust and truth into political weapons; Horikita adopts an anti-discard principle while independently outmaneuvering Kushida; Ayanokōji demonstrates both autonomy-supportive restraint and a hidden removal contingency.

## Revision status

The second pass strengthens the prior “weaponized truth and refusal to discard” reading but broadens it into **integration versus removal**. It also corrects the shorthand that Ayanokōji personally plants cheating evidence: the source strongly supports his design of the cheating-frame contingency, while direct physical placement remains inferential.


# Y1V07 — Volume 7

## Volume thesis

Volume 7 stages a conflict among paternal ownership, rule-bound sanctuary, fear sovereignty, and protection sovereignty. Ayanokōji rejects being owned while deliberately intensifying Kei’s dependency; Kei’s refusal exceeds both Ryūen’s fear model and Ayanokōji’s minimum script.

## Revision status

The second pass retains the first-pass ownership/fear/protection thesis but sharpens it through **futurity and developmental sovereignty**. It corrects the treatment of the Matsuo account as independently verified fact, rejects a globally emotionless Ayanokōji reading, and identifies Kei’s refusal as autonomous choice inside a nonconsensually authored crisis.



# Y1V07.5 — Volume 7.5

## Volume thesis

Christmas functions as relational counter-curriculum. Ayanokōji is offered transparent ordinary romance through Satō but preserves Kei as the person most compatible with his hidden strategic world. Kei moves from coerced protected collaboration toward a negotiated protected partnership, while the opening question of emotional irreplaceability remains answered only by functional indispensability.

# Y1V08 — Volume 8

## Volume thesis

Volume 8 makes trust simultaneously a moral relation, a form of political capital, and an institutional attack surface. The Mixed Training Camp produces real temporary community through coerced proximity, yet Nagumo's year-wide organization converts rule influence, credibility, liquidity, gender separation, leader liability, and coordinated testimony into a targeted operation against Tachibana and Manabu. Manabu wins the announced score contest; Nagumo wins the political result. Ayanokōji remains selectively active while formalizing a private hierarchy of people he will and will not protect.

## Revision status

- The prior reading of Volume 8 as the emergence of Nagumo's institutional politics is **STRENGTHENED**: Ayanokōji explicitly reconstructs that the strategy preceded and helped produce the examination.
- Manabu's distance is **CORRECTED** from simple emotional austerity into a recognized leadership blind spot whose cost is political as well as personal.
- Ayanokōji's withdrawal is **CORRECTED** from generalized passivity into selective nonintervention, surveillance, low-coercion assistance, and private protection.
- The mixed group is **NEWLY STRENGTHENED** as evidence that genuine community and distributed development can emerge inside a coercive institutional container without being fully authored by Ayanokōji.
- Horikita's Kushida project is **STRENGTHENED** as autonomous judgment, while remaining ethically and strategically unresolved.
# Y1V09 — Volume 9

## Volume thesis

Volume 9 asks who possesses the right to determine what a true fact means about a person. Ichinose’s past shoplifting remains a real wrong, but Sakayanagi attempts to convert that fact into permanent moral identity; Ayanokōji counters not by restoring a clean information environment, but by saturating the school with additional rumors until institutional regulation becomes unavoidable. The result preserves Ichinose’s capacity to continue becoming while exposing the ethical danger of rescuing another person through authored reputation and coercive developmental pressure.

## Revision status

The first-pass “guilt and forgiveness” reading is retained but materially strengthened. The second pass adds information-environment authorship, Nagumo’s proprietary use of trust, the staged nature of the Kamuro proof, the ethical cost of rumor saturation, and the dual character of Ayanokōji’s “door” intervention as both genuine nonjudgmental listening and deliberate pressure.


# Y1V10 — Volume 10

## Volume thesis

The Class Vote converts continued membership into a deliberately scarce institutional good. Competing class constitutions reveal incompatible theories of legitimate human valuation, while the Tsukishiro ending shows that the supposedly neutral procedure may itself be captured by adults who have already selected a target.

# Y1V11 — Volume 11

## Volume thesis

Volume 11 tests ability at the point where development, coordination, display, and official record diverge. Horikita builds distributed class capacity; Ayanokōji makes another person’s development an explicitly self-chosen project; Sakayanagi revises her hereditarian theory through direct encounter; and Tsukishiro demonstrates that institutional sovereignty can overwrite the displayed result itself. The volume therefore advances the Year 1 conflict from **developmental authorship** to **procedural capture**.

## Revision status

- **STRENGTHENED:** Horikita’s independence and distributed leadership; she increasingly produces and coordinates capacities Ayanokōji does not possess personally.
- **STRENGTHENED:** Ayanokōji’s desire for a genuine contest and for growth that may exceed his own model.
- **CORRECTED:** the chess climax is not a single clean hierarchy. Official result, counterfactual live action, and private replay must be separated.
- **STRENGTHENED / ETHICALLY DARKENED:** developmental authorship becomes explicitly self-chosen, while the Hirata intervention demonstrates that self-authorship does not guarantee legitimate method.
- **NEWLY FOUND:** institutional `recorded ability`—the officially preserved result can diverge from possessed, displayed, or measured ability when administrative infrastructure is captured.
- **UNRESOLVED:** Sakayanagi’s retraction of `偽りの天才` revises her personal classification of Ayanokōji, but the volume does not settle nature versus nurture as an authorial scientific doctrine.

# Y1V11.5 — Volume 11.5

## Volume thesis

Volume 11.5 closes the numbered/decimal Year 1 novel sequence by changing the governing problem from self-authorship into **reciprocal authorship**. Horikita integrates rather than purifies the self shaped by imitation; Ayanokōji identifies ordinary high-school life as his first self-chosen goal, then discovers that developing other students may itself be a self-chosen purpose; Manabu frames legacy without prescribing imitation; and the Kei confession combines explicit reciprocity with a hidden developmental curriculum whose ethical status remains unresolved.

## Final-lock revision status

- **STRENGTHENED / SPLIT:** Ayanokōji's freedom thread separates into ordinary-life self-authorship, self-chosen developmental purpose, and the unresolved problem of reciprocal authorship.
- **CORRECTED:** Horikita's haircut and farewell are integration of a lived imitative self, not recovery of a pristine pre-influence identity.
- **STRENGTHENED / UNRESOLVED:** Ayanokōji–Kei becomes explicit mutual romance, while `教科書`, dependency-cure framing, hoped-for `掛け替えのない存在`, and final `祈ろう` prevent a simplistic completed-humanization reading.
- **REFINED:** Ichinose receives a genuine future horizon and interpersonal trust, while `介錯` preserves Ayanokōji's terminal developmental judgment.
- **NEWLY FOREGROUNDED:** legacy, reciprocal authorship, hidden curriculum, self-concealed ability beyond Ayanokōji, and adult institutional conscience.

# Y1FF — *First File* paratext

## Paratext thesis

*First File* adds a final epistemic layer to Year 1: **the file and the person**. Its data, ratings, editorial summaries, and bonus fiction are not interchangeable source types. The institutional file may accurately record bounded visible output while remaining radically incomplete as a total model of the person. The two bonus stories then return the archive to ordinary desire, relational ambiguity, and competitive agency that cannot be exhausted by the file.

## Final-lock source authority note

- `FF-DATA`: strong evidence for what the official guidebook records about institutional state.
- `FF-RATING`: strong evidence for the dated/counterfactual metric itself, not proof that the metric exhausts the person.
- `FF-EDITORIAL`: official retrospective framing; useful but compression-prone.
- `FF-FICTION`: narrative fiction and the strongest literary layer inside *First File*.
- Fixed-layout documentary wording remains image-primary. OCR remains retrieval-only.

# Final Year 1 evidence-lock statement

The canonical Year 1 evidence ledger now contains **1,419 unique evidence IDs** across fourteen Japanese numbered/decimal novels and the *First File* mixed paratext audit. No Year 2 novel, Volume 0, *Second List*, Year 3, adaptation, wiki, or later-franchise evidence has been admitted into the Year 1 source boundary.

# Specialist-corpus finalization audit — frozen routing layer

## Purpose

The 1,419 evidence records above were already source-locked before the Year 1 specialist synthesis sequence was completed. This finalization pass does **not** generate replacement evidence, renumber IDs, delete low-reuse rows, rewrite source locators, or alter the claim/event field of any canonical row. Its job is narrower:

1. verify that the frozen specialist corpus `Y1_01–Y1_11` is itself the canonical input set;
2. expand every inline evidence range used by those documents and confirm that every referenced ID resolves to this ledger;
3. distinguish evidence that is explicitly reused in specialist synthesis from evidence retained primarily for source-local retrieval;
4. add routing metadata for source-local records that are not explicitly cited by a frozen specialist document;
5. freeze this file as the definitive Year 1 scene-level evidence router before the terminology/index audit and continuous full-year synthesis.

The governing invariant is:

> **Evidence existence is not determined by synthesis reuse. A source-grounded record remains canonical even when no specialist document needs to cite it explicitly.**

These hotspots disproportionately concern the late-Year-1 convergence of developmental authorship, class-vote ethics, Tsukishiro/record sovereignty, Horikita self-authorship, romance, ordinary life, and *First File*'s file/person distinction. They should be treated as **crossroads**, not as substitutes for the surrounding source-local evidence.

## Routing principles for the full synthesis

The continuous Year 1 synthesis should use this ledger according to five rules.

### 1. Route through the topical home first

When a specialist document already owns the argument, the full synthesis should normally cite that specialist for interpretation and this ledger for scene-level evidence rather than recreating an independent mini-essay.

### 2. Use evidence IDs as stable joins

The stable join is:

`full synthesis claim → specialist topical home → evidence ID → deterministic source locator → Japanese primary source`

For *First File* documentary pages, the final join remains image-primary under the source-audit rules.

### 3. Preserve question-relative authority

An `FF-RATING` record can be strong evidence for a rating without becoming strong evidence for whole-person ability. An `FF-EDITORIAL` record can establish official retrospective framing without erasing causal ambiguity preserved by novel scenes. A character's testimony can be strong evidence that the testimony occurred without becoming independent proof that every asserted fact is true.

### 4. Do not reward citation density

A claim supported by one precise high-authority scene may be stronger than a claim supported by ten loosely relevant records. Counts are audit metadata, not epistemic weights.

### 5. Preserve unused capacity

The 39 source-local-only records remain available to challenge or refine the full synthesis. Specialist omission must never be treated as evidence against relevance if a later integrative claim makes the record material.

## Final Year 1 evidence-router lock

This file is now the definitive scene-level evidence router for the completed Year 1 specialist corpus. The canonical evidence set remains exactly **1,419 records**:

- **1,337** Japanese-novel evidence records;
- **82** *First File* mixed-paratext records;
- **15** source units with contiguous local numbering;
- **0** duplicate IDs;
- **0** unresolved specialist references;
- **0** malformed specialist evidence ranges.

No Year 2 novel, Volume 0, *Second List*, Year 3, adaptation, wiki, or later-franchise evidence has entered this lock.

Future corrections to evidence identity or locator integrity must be issued as an explicit later revision with a change log. They must not silently renumber, delete, or repurpose existing IDs.
